<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * items and into nested sub-items; when a submenu folds, or the whole sidebar</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * items get tooltips. ⌘/Ctrl+B toggles the most recently mounted or focused</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * sidebar on the page (one shared listener, not one per instance).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// ζ ≈ 1.09: a slightly overdamped settle with no bounce.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> WIDTH_SPRING</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"spring"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, stiffness: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">380</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, damping: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">36</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, mass: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0.7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> PILL_SPRING</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"spring"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, stiffness: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">420</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, damping: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">36</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, mass: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0.7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> EASE</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0.22</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0.36</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/* ───────────────────────── Shortcut registry ───────────────────────── */</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// One window listener for every SidebarProvider on the page. Instances stack in</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// mount order; focusing or pressing inside one moves it to the top, and only the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// topmost instance with a matching key handles ⌘/Ctrl+key.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> HotkeyEntry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">key</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">run</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> void</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> hotkeyStack</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> HotkeyEntry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> []</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> onHotkey</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> KeyboardEvent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(e.metaKey </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> e.ctrlKey) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> e.defaultPrevented) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> key</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> e.key.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">toLowerCase</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> hotkeyStack.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">>=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">--</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (hotkeyStack[i].key </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> key) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">continue</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    e.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">preventDefault</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    hotkeyStack[i].</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">run</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> SidebarProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">typeof</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> motion.aside>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"children"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  side</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "left"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "right"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** `floating`: raised rounded card; `sidebar`: flush with a hairline edge. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  variant</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "floating"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "sidebar"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** `icon` folds into an icon rail; `none` never collapses. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  collapsible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "icon"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "none"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  panelClassName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> data-slot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"sidebar-header"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">cn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 p-2"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, className)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>
